<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__130.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 48th (2021) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>48th (2021)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 13-11, BCO 38-1, RAO 16-3.e.8" href="exc/korean-southwest__130.html">BCO 13-11, BCO 38-1, RAO 16-3.e.8</a><p class="rpr-card__body">The minutes approved by the presbytery and submitted to RPR do not include a full and accurate record of investigation, confession and deposition of a TE, including a written statement of confession recorded in the TE’s own words, or a statement of facts as required by BCO 38-1.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 13-11, BCO 38-1, RAO 16-3.e.8</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> raised (48th)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> raised (open)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>

<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__210.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 11-4, BCO 15-1" href="exc/korean-southwest__210.html">BCO 11-4, BCO 15-1</a><p class="rpr-card__body">Article 23 of the By-Laws contains an unconstitutional provision. Presbytery may not mandate the length of training a Session decides to employ for new officers. It can only review minutes to be sure training and examinations were accomplished ( BCO 11-4; 241). [Note: Presbytery’s response to a previous exception stated that this was corrected, but it is not reflected in the bylaws submitted to GA.].</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 11-4, BCO 15-1</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> satisfactory (52nd) → satisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> satisfactory (closed)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>

<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__219.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 13-6, BCO 21-4, BCO 23-1" href="exc/korean-southwest__219.html">BCO 13-6, BCO 21-4, BCO 23-1</a><p class="rpr-card__body">There is no evidence in the minutes as to whether men examined by Presbytery had stated differences to the Westminster Standards, and, if they did, how Presbytery judged those. ( BCO 13-6; 21-4). <article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__223.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 13-6" href="exc/korean-southwest__223.html">BCO 13-6</a><p class="rpr-card__body">No record of written credentials of dismissing Presbytery (not specified) for TE transfer.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 13-6</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> unsatisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> unsatisfactory (outstanding)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__224.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 13-9, BCO 5-2.b, BCO 13-8" href="exc/korean-southwest__224.html">BCO 13-9, BCO 5-2.b, BCO 13-8</a><p class="rpr-card__body">Under credentials committee report, no record of acting on recommenations to: 1) approval of dissolution of church; 2) change call of TE; and 3) TE request to withdraw from Presbyery.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 13-9, BCO 5-2.b, BCO 13-8</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> raised (52nd) → raised (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> raised (open)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__225.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 15-1" href="exc/korean-southwest__225.html">BCO 15-1</a><p class="rpr-card__body">Minutes show that commissions met and who was present, but there are no reports showing what the commissions did.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 15-1</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> satisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> satisfactory (closed)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__226.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 15-1" href="exc/korean-southwest__226.html">BCO 15-1</a><p class="rpr-card__body">A commission must be appointed by Presbytery, not a committee.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 15-1</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> unsatisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> unsatisfactory (outstanding)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__227.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 18-2" href="exc/korean-southwest__227.html">BCO 18-2</a><p class="rpr-card__body">No record of endorsement by candidates’ Sessions, six-months membership for candidates, nor of charge given to candidates.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 18-2</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> unsatisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> unsatisfactory (outstanding)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>

<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__230.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 19-2, RAO 16-3.e.5" href="exc/korean-southwest__230.html">BCO 19-2, RAO 16-3.e.5</a><p class="rpr-card__body">All specific requirements of three licensure exams not recorded. <article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__231.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 19-2.e-f" href="exc/korean-southwest__231.html">BCO 19-2.e-f</a><p class="rpr-card__body">No record that Presbytery judged the stated differences of licensure candidates during their examination.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 19-2.e-f</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> raised (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> raised (open)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__232.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 19-2.f, BCO 5-2.b, BCO 13-8" href="exc/korean-southwest__232.html">BCO 19-2.f, BCO 5-2.b, BCO 13-8</a><p class="rpr-card__body">Presbytery apparently received organized churches without sessions and without providing rationale.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 19-2.f, BCO 5-2.b, BCO 13-8</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> raised (52nd) → raised (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> raised (open)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__233.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 19-3, BCO 12-5.b" href="exc/korean-southwest__233.html">BCO 19-3, BCO 12-5.b</a><p class="rpr-card__body">Presbytery overstepped its constitutional authority in approving a church&#x27;s request to increase Ruling Elders. The number of Ruling Elders is to be determined by the church&#x27;s members, not the Presbytery ( BCO 24-1).</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 19-3, BCO 12-5.b</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> raised (52nd) → raised (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> raised (open)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__234.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 19-7" href="exc/korean-southwest__234.html">BCO 19-7</a><p class="rpr-card__body">Presbytery uses candidacy examination procedures for internship, so various steps are missing, including determination of the nature of the internship.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 19-7</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> satisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> satisfactory (closed)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__235.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 20, BCO 21-4, RAO 16-3.e.5" href="exc/korean-southwest__235.html">BCO 20, BCO 21-4, RAO 16-3.e.5</a><p class="rpr-card__body">Article 22 of the ByLaws contains an unconstitutional provision. <article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__236.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 20-1" href="exc/korean-southwest__236.html">BCO 20-1</a><p class="rpr-card__body">no record of call to a definite work.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 20-1</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> unsatisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> unsatisfactory (outstanding)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__237.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 20-1, BCO 8-10" href="exc/korean-southwest__237.html">BCO 20-1, BCO 8-10</a><p class="rpr-card__body">Teaching elders are received into presbytery with no evidence of a call to a definite work ( BCO 20-1, paragraph 1). <article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__238.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 20-1" href="exc/korean-southwest__238.html">BCO 20-1</a><p class="rpr-card__body">No record of the reasons why Presbytery considers an out-of-bounds work to be a valid Christian ministry.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 20-1</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> raised (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> raised (open)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>

<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__252.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 30, BCO 38-3" href="exc/korean-southwest__252.html">BCO 30, BCO 38-3</a><p class="rpr-card__body">Men are removed from the membership of Presbytery apart from methods set forth in the BCO . This problem also exists in Chapter 5, paragraphs 3-4 of Presbytery’s By-Laws. ( BCO 30 spells out censures that Presbytery may apply after a case without process ( BCO 38) ora case of process ( BCO 31, 32, 34, 35, 36) or via transfer ( BCO 20-9). No record that congregations concurred with dissolution of pastoral relations, that Presbytery dissolved the pastoral relations, nor that Presbytery voted to release the TEs to the different Presbyteries.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 46-6</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> unsatisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> unsatisfactory (outstanding)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__258.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 5-9" href="exc/korean-southwest__258.html">BCO 5-9</a><p class="rpr-card__body">Presbytery particularizes mission works with no evidence that all required procedures were followed – specifically no evidence of congregational meeting or petition.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 5-9</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> unsatisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> unsatisfactory (outstanding)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__259.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 5-9.g-h" href="exc/korean-southwest__259.html">BCO 5-9.g-h</a><p class="rpr-card__body">Presbytery appears to have particularized a mission church without receiving and approving a petition from its members.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 5-9.g-h</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> raised (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> raised (open)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__260.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 7-3, BCO 8-1" href="exc/korean-southwest__260.html">BCO 7-3, BCO 8-1</a><p class="rpr-card__body">Presbytery has yet to take sufficient action on a report of an important delinquency of one of its member churches which apparently permitted &quot;an ordained woman to be on staff.&quot; The woman is listed on the church&#x27;s website with the title of &quot;pastor&quot; ( 목사 ).</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 7-3, BCO 8-1</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> raised (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> raised (open)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__261.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 8-4, BCO 13-10" href="exc/korean-southwest__261.html">BCO 8-4, BCO 13-10</a><p class="rpr-card__body">No record that Presbytery gave at least 60-days notice of decision to dissolve a church.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 8-4, BCO 13-10</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> raised (52nd) → raised (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> raised (open)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__262.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 8-4, BCO 15-1, RAO 16-3.e.4" href="exc/korean-southwest__262.html">BCO 8-4, BCO 15-1, RAO 16-3.e.4</a><p class="rpr-card__body">Presbytery apparently approves calls for men to work outside the bounds of Presbytery, but does not record in the minutes why these are needful works and valid Christian ministries, nor do the minutes demonstrate that there is assurance the man will have full freedom in preaching and teaching ( BCO 8-4; 8-7; 20-1) For each man received into Presbytery in 2023 on the basis of a call to a work outside the jurisdiction of Presbytery, Presbytery must now approve and include in its minutes a statement of why the work is a needful work and a valid Christian ministry, and how Presbytery has been assured the man will have full freedom to preach and teach the doctrine of our Church.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 8-4, BCO 15-1, RAO 16-3.e.4</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> satisfactory (52nd) → satisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> satisfactory (closed)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__263.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 8-7, BCO 21-4.g, RAO 16-3.e.5" href="exc/korean-southwest__263.html">BCO 8-7, BCO 21-4.g, RAO 16-3.e.5</a><p class="rpr-card__body">it is unclear if presbytery approved TE to labor out of bounds.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 8-7, BCO 21-4.g, RAO 16-3.e.5</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> raised (52nd) → raised (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> raised (open)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__264.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 8-7" href="exc/korean-southwest__264.html">BCO 8-7</a><p class="rpr-card__body">No record of annual reports of TEs laboring out of bounds.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 8-7</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> satisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> satisfactory (closed)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__265.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 8-7" href="exc/korean-southwest__265.html">BCO 8-7</a><p class="rpr-card__body">No record that the Presbytery is assured that an out-of-bounds TE will have full freedom to maintain and teach the doctrine of our Church.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 8-7</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> satisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> satisfactory (closed)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__266.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 8-7" href="exc/korean-southwest__266.html">BCO 8-7</a><p class="rpr-card__body">A TE has begun to minister out of bounds. No record that Presbytery followed the appropriate BCO provisions to approve that.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 8-7</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> unsatisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> unsatisfactory (outstanding)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
<article class="home-result home-result--rpr rpr-card" data-result-item data-result-url="exc/korean-southwest__267.html" data-result-title="Korean Southwest — 52nd (2025) RPR exception" data-result-type="RPR exception"><span class="home-result__metadata"><span class="home-result__category">RPR exception</span><span class="home-result__separator" aria-hidden="true">•</span><span>52nd (2025)</span></span><a class="home-result__title" data-result-primary data-result-type="RPR exception" data-result-title="BCO 8-7" href="exc/korean-southwest__267.html">BCO 8-7</a><p class="rpr-card__body">TEs are reported as resigning from or having left current calls from churches of Presbytery and/or are recorded as taking new calls, but no evidence that Presbytery dissolved pastoral relations, nor acted on the new calls, nor is there evidence that 8-7 and 21-1 were followed for out of bounds calls.</p><span class="home-result__facts"><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Provision(s):</b> BCO 8-7</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Lifecycle:</b> unsatisfactory (52nd)</span><span class="home-result__fact"><b>Final disposition:</b> unsatisfactory (outstanding)</span></span><details class="result-actions"><summary>Actions</summary><div class="result-actions__panel" aria-label="Record actions"><button type="button" data-result-action="save">Save</button><button type="button" data-result-action="cite">Cite</button><button type="button" data-result-action="link">Copy link</button></div></details></article>
